karae ha. dosto jaisa kaplog jantay hain ky jin provinces main upgradation hoi han us ky pechay jado jehaad ki ek lambi dastan ha. wo kehty hain na ky jab tak bacha roye na to maan bhi doodh nahi deti iss liye kabhi bhi koe plate main rakh krapko apna haq nahi dy ga apna haq lainay ky liye himat or jazba darkar han jis ka izhar kpk or punjab ky dosto ny kya or kamyabi hasil ki. dosto hamaray liyeaplogoon ki ray ki bohat importance han iss liye hum aaj sy apna protest start krny ka elaan kartay hein. iss silsilay main pahla protest 14th january 2016 at 11:00 aam apca (pakistan railway's wing) ky zaer-e-ehtemaam bamaqam pakistan railways divisional superintendent office, rawalpindi opposite rawalpindi railway station, ho ga. tamam dosto (especially islamabad secretariat employees association, apca bait-ul-mal & iesco employees association) sy request han ky apnay haq ky liye bahir nikly or iss ehtajaaj main apni shirkat yaqeeni banain keun ky agar ab bhi hum na nikly to phir yai mamlaa yn he latka rahay ga.insha allah for more details please contact: protest co-ordinator 0300-2341366 #primeministeroffice #pmpakistan #mianmuhammadnawazsharif #pmajk #sardarabdulmajid
